54b3(phal chen): phyin pa sgo kun dang ldan pas/thams cad mkhyen pa'i sgor 'jug pas shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa yongs su dag par byed do//'jig rten gyi bya ba'i rgyud thams cad ston pa dang /sems can gyi rgyud yongs su 
54b4(phal chen): smin pas yongs su mi skyo ba dang /bsam pa bzhin du bya ba ston pa dang /bya ba thams cad la mngon par chags pa med pa dang /byis pa'i spyod pa dang /'phags pa'i spyod pa bsam pa ji lta ba bzhin 
54b5(phal chen): du ston pa dang /'khor ba dang mya ngan las 'das pa nye bar ston pa dang /sbyor ba thams cad shin tu brtags pas rgyu ba dang /rgyan thams cad bstan pa gnas pa med pa dang /'gro ba thams cad kyi rdzas su 
54b6(phal chen): 'gro zhing rjes su 'brang ba dang /sems can thams cad sgrol ba'i spyod pas thabs mkhas pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa yongs du sbyong ngo //sems can gyi khams mthar thug par yongs su smin par bya ba dang /'jig 
54b7(phal chen): rten gyi khams mthar thug pa yongs su sbyang ba dang /de bzhin gshegs pa mthar thug par mchod pa dang /chos chags pa med pa'i mthar thug par rnam par 'tshang rgya ba dang /chos kyi dbyings kyi mthar 
54b8(phal chen): thug pa'i spyod pa'i dkyil 'khor yongs su rdzogs par bya ba dang /bskal pa phyi ma'i mthar thug par rab tu gnas pa dang /sems bskyed pa'i mthar thug par stobs la rab tu 'jug pa dang /'gro ba 
55a1(phal chen): @#//dang ldog pa mthar thug par mngon par 'tshang rgya ba dang /zhing gi gnas kyi mthar thug par thams cad mthong ba dang /de bzhin gshegs pa'i ye shes kyi mthar thug par btu 
55a2(phal chen): thob par bya bas smon lam gyi pha rol tu phyin pa yongs su dag par byed do//bsam pa'i stobs kun nas nyon mongs pa med pa dang /lhag pa'i bsam pa'i stobs dbang du gyur cing thub pa med pa dang /
55a3(phal chen): snying rje chen po'i stobs skyo ba med pa dang /byams pa chen po'i stobs snyoms pa'i sbyor ba can dang /gzungs kyi stobs don thams cad kyi sbyor ba la sgrib pa med pa dang /spobs pa'i stobs 'gro ba 
55a4(phal chen): thams cad tshim par byed pa dang /pha rol tu phyin pa'i stobs theg pa thams cad kyi rgyan byed pa dang /smon lam gyi stobs bcad du mi rung ba dang /mngon par shes pa'i stobs tshad med pa mngon 
55a5(phal chen): par bsgrub pa dang /sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi brlabs la mos pa dam pa'i stobs rab tu 'dzin pas/stobs kyi pha rol tu phyin pa longs su dag par byed do/'dod chags kyi spyod pa khong du chud pa dang /zhe 
55a6(phal chen): sdang gi spyod pa rjes su rtogs pa dang /gti mug gi spyod pa rab tu rtogs pa dang /cha mnyam pa'i spyod pa rjes su shes pa dang /slob pa'i sems can gyi spyod pa rab tu rtogs pa la/skad cig cig gis 
55a7(phal chen): sems can mtha' yas pa'i spyod pa rnam par rtog pa dang /sems dang bsam pa mtha' yas pa thams cad shes par byed pa dang /chos thams cad la yang dag par ji lta ba bzhin du rtogs pa dang /de 
55a8(phal chen): bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs kyi rjes su 'jug pa dang /chos kyi dbyings kyi sgo thams cad dang ldan pa khong du chud pas/ye shes kyi pha rol tu phyin pa yongs su dag par byed do//de dag de ltar pha rol tu 
55b1(phal chen): phyin pa rnams yongs su sbyong zhing pha rol tu phyin pa de dag yongs su rdzogs par byed pa dang /'di lta bu phyin ci dmigs pa mi gtong bar byang chub sems dpa'i theg pa rgyan chen po'i rgyan dang ldan pa la gnas shing //
55b2(phal chen): sems can gyi khams ji ltar dam bcas pa bzhin du yang dag par sgrol lo//de dag dge ba'i las shin tu bsags pa ston pas/ngan song du 'gro ba'i sems can sgrol lo//brtsams pa chen po la gzung bas sems 
55b3(phal chen): can gyi mi khom par 'gro ba sgrol lo//lhag mthong nye bar stob pas gzugs kyi khams na 'khod pa'i sems can gyi khams sgrol lo//phra ba'i ye shes nye bar stob pas gzugs med pa'i khams su skyes pa'i sems 
55b4(phal chen): can rnams sgrol lo//zhi ba'i tshul nye bar stobs pas nyan thos dang rang sangs rgyas kyi theg pa la mos pa'i sems can gyi khams sgral lo//stobs bcu'i rgyan chen po dang ldan pa bstabs pas/theg pa chen po 
55b5(phal chen): la mos pa'i sems can gyis khams sgrol lo//de ltar na sngon las dang po pa'i sa la gnas pa'i tshe/sems can ga langs chen po nas ltung ba rnam pa tha dad par gyur pa ji lta ba bzhin du shes te/sgo sna tshogs nas 
55b6(phal chen): gzung bar bya'o//zhes seng+ge'i sgra sgrags pa de bzhin du ye shes thob pa rnams thams cad bsgral ba'i phyir rab tu zhugs pa yin no//kye rgyal ba'i sras byang chub sems dpa' ye shes de lta bu la zhugs pa ni 
55b7(phal chen): rtag tu rgyun mi gcod par sangs rgyas kyi rigs dang chos kyi rigs dang dge 'dun gyi rigs mi gcad pa'i phyir zhugs pa yin te/de dag byang chub kyi sems gzhan gyi rgyud la gzhol bas sangs rgyas kyi rigs mi 
55b8(phal chen): gcod pa yin/chos kyi mdzod rnam par 'grel pa ma chos kyi rigs mi gcod pa yin/bstan pa'i tshul dang 'gal bar mi byed pas/dge 'dun gyi rigs mi gcod pa yin/smon lam chen po yang dag par 
56a1(phal chen): @#//brjod pas sangs rgyas kyi rigs mi gcod pa yin/rten cing 'brel bar bstung ba'i tshul yang dag par ston pas chos kyi rigs mi gcod pa yi na/snying po thob par byed pa'i chos la brtson pas 
56a2(phal chen): dge 'dun gyi rigs mi gcod pa yin/de bzhin gshegs pa'i sa bon sems can gyi zhing la 'debs pas/sangs rgyas kyi rigs mi gcad pa yin/dam pa'i chos yongs su 'dzin pas chos kyi rigs mi 
56a3(phal chen): gcod pa yin/chags chen po yongs su sdud pas mi skyobs dge 'dun gyi rigs mi gcad pa yin/'das pa dang ma byon pa dang /da ltar byung ba'i sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyis mdzad pa dran 
56a4(phal chen): pa'i phyir/rtag tu de bzhin gshegs pa'i gdams ngag dang /rjes su bstan pa mi gtong bas/sangs rgyas dang chos dang dge 'dun gyi rigs mi bzod pa yin/de dag de ltar skyabs gsum gyi rigs mi 
56a5(phal chen): gcad pa'i phyir zhugs pas rtsom pa thams cad kha na ma tho ba med pa yin te/brtsams pa gang ci rtsom yang rung /de dag thams cad du thogs pa med par thams cad mkhyen pa'i sgor 'byung ba thob par byed do//de 
56a6(phal chen): dag de ltar lus kyi las kha na ma tho ba med pa dang /ngag gi las kha na ma tho ba med ba dang /yid kyi las kha na ma tho ba me dpal gnas pas dge ba'i rtsa ba brtsom pa don yod par brtsom pa yin te/sems can gyi 
56a7(phal chen): don sbyor ba don yod par brtsom mo//sems can don yod par 'dul bar byed do//chos don yod par ston to//de'i chos ston pa thams cad kyang sems can gyi bsam pa tshim par bya ba la thogs pa med pas 
56a8(phal chen): khong du chud par 'gyur ro//de'i sems bskyed pa gcig tsam yang log par mi 'gyur te/sems bskyed pa gzhan thabs dang shes rab kyis yongs su zin pa thams cad kyang thams cad mkhyen pa nyid du 
56b1(phal chen): yongs su bsngo'o//de de ltar chos dkar po bsags pa don yod pa lta bu la gnas pas rgyan rnam par phye ba bcus sems bskyed pa thams cad yongs su bsngo ste/bcu gang zhe na 'di ltar lus kyi rgyan sems can 
56b2(phal chen): bsam pa ji lta ba bzhin du yang dag par ston pa dang /ngag gi rgyan the tsom thams cad gcod pa dang /sems kyi rgyan skad cig gcig gis ting nge 'dzin rjes su rtogs par byed pa dang /sangs rgyas kyi zhing gi rgyan 
56b3(phal chen): kun nas nyon mongs pa med pa dang /'od kyi rgyan 'od zer gyi dra ba mtha' dang dbus med pa bye ba bsgo ba thams cad mkhyen pa dang /'khor gyi dkyil 'khor gyi rgyan sems can gyi rnam pa tshad med pa'i 
56b4(phal chen): tshogs yongs su mgu bar byed pa dang /rdzu 'phrul rnam par 'phrul pa'i rgyan sems can gyi bsam pa ji lta ba bzhin du/rdzu 'phrul gyis rnam par 'phrul pa dang /bstan pa'i rgyan mkhas pa'i skye bo thams 
56b5(phal chen): cad kyis yongs su gzung ba dang /mya ngan las 'das pa'i rgyan mngon par sangs rgyas pa gcig cig gis phyogs bcu ma lus par rgyas par 'gengs pa dang /chos byin gyis brlabs pa'i rgyan yul dang /dus 
56b6(phal chen): dang snod ji lta ba bzhin du sdod pa dag ste/de dag rgyan de lta bus sems bskyed pa thams cad du mngon par bsgrub pas lus dang ngag dang yid kyi las don yod pa dang /ye shes chen po don yod pa rab tu 'thob 
56b7(phal chen): ste|de dag mthong bas kyang 'di ltar bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu yongs su smin par 'gyur ba'i phyir sems can rnams la don yod pa yin te/thos pa dang bsnyen bkur ba dang /'grogs 
56b8(phal chen): pa dang /rjes su dran pa dang /rjes su rab tu byung ba dang /chos ston pa dang /dge ba'i rtsa ba la rjes su yid dang nga ba dang /dad cing mos pa dang /chung ngu ni ming brjod pa tsam yang 'di ltar bla na med pa la yang dag par 
57a1(phal chen): @#//rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu yongs su smin pa'i phyir sems can rnams la don du 'gyur ba yin no//kye rgyal ba'i sras 'di lta ste dper na rtsi'i ming mthong na bde ba zhes bya ba de mthong ma 
57a2(phal chen): thag tu sems can rnams kyi lus la zhen pa'am ltor zos pa'i dug rab tu zhi bar 'gyur ro//kye rgyal ba'i sras de bzhin chos de lta bu dang ldan pa'i byang chub sems dpa' yang mthong ma thag tu sems 
57a3(phal chen): can rnams kyi nyon mongs pa'i dug thams cad byang bar 'gyur te/chos dkar po'i stobs kyi shugs kyang skye bar 'gyur ro//kye rgyalaba'i sras de ltar byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po chos nyid 
57a4(phal chen): de lta bu dang ldan zhing chos de dag la gnas te/shes rab kyi 'od chen pos ma rig pa'i mun pa bsal nas/bdud kyi dkrugs ma'i 'khor thams cad byams pa dang /snying rje chen po'i stobs kyi pham 
57a5(phal chen): par byas te/ye shes dang bsod nams kyi stobs kyi shugs kyi phas kyi rgol ba thams cad kyi zil gyis mnan nas/rdo rje lta bu'i ting nge 'dzin la 'jug pas/sems kyi dri ma thams cad dang /
57a6(phal chen): nyon mongs pa'i phyogs thams cad bsal nas/skye ba med pa'i stobs dang shugs kyis dge ba'i rtsa ba phun sum tshogs pa thams cad rab tu bsgrubs te/sngon chad sangs rgyas kyi zhing yongs su dag 
57a7(phal chen): par spyad pa'i stobs dang shugs gyis mi khom pa dang /ngan song dang ngan 'gro'i lam thams cad rnam par bzlog nas/mngon par zhen pa med pa'i stobs dang shug kyis ye shes kyi dkyil 'khor 
57a8(phal chen): yongs su sbyangs te/rim gyis thabs dang shes rab kyi stobs dang /shugs kyis byang chub sems dpa'i sa dang pha rol tu phyin pa dang /ting nge 'dzin dang /mngon par shes pa dang /rig pa dang mi 
57b1(phal chen): 'jig pa yongs su dag pa mngon par bsgrubs nas/chos dkar po thams cad kyi stobs dang shugs kyis sangs rgyas thams cad kyi mtshan dang dpe byad bzang po dang /sku dang gsung dang thugs kyi rgyan yongs su 
57b2(phal chen): dag pa mngon par bsgrub pa rab tu rgyas par byas ste/ye shes kyis shes par bya ba'i dbang po dang /byin gyis brlabs kyis de bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs dang /sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa thams cad 
57b3(phal chen): mnyam pa nyid khong du chud par byas nas/bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub kyang myur du 'thob cing thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes kyi yul yang smon lam gyi khyad par ji lta ba bzhin du 'thob par 
57b4(phal chen): 'gyur ro//sngon gyi byin gyis brlabs dang /ye shes kyi byin gyi brlabs kyis mngon par sangs rgyas nas/sems can rnams kyi bsam pa ji lta ba bzhin/sangs rgyas kyi zhing ston te/'dul ba ji lta ba bzhin du 
57b5(phal chen): sems can rnams la chos kyi 'khor lo chen po bskor cing /mtha' yas pa'i sems can rnam par 'dul lo//kye rgyal ba'i sras de ltar byang chub sems dpa'i chos de lta bu dang ldan pa dag tshul de lta bus sangs 
57b6(phal chen): rgyas thams cad dang mnyam pa nyid kyi brang du thob par 'gyur te/byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa thob nas kyang /'jig rten gyi khams mtha' yas pa dag tu dam pa'i chos yongs su 'dzin cing /chos smra ba chen po yang 
57b7(phal chen): byed do//de ltar chos smra ba byed pa'i tshe de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi brlabs pa de dag chos kyi mdzod chen po gzung zhing bsrung ba'i phyir/tha dad pa yang dag 
57b8(phal chen): par shes pa'i chos kyi tshul rab tu dbye ba'i rjes su song ba'i byang chub sems dpa'i tshig gis 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor mtha' yas par bdag gi lus kyis rgyan byin gyi brlabs kyis bstan pa mngon par bsgrub 
58a1(phal chen): @#//pa'i rnam pa'i mchog thams cad dang ldan pa/tha dad pa yang dag par shes pa'i mkhas pa mtha' yas pa dang /ye shes mi zad pa'i sgor 'jug pa dang /dbyings kyi dkyil 'khor shin tu 
58a2(phal chen): rnam par phye ba'i byin gyi brlabs pa dang /dbyangs gcig bsgrags pas 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor thams cad mgu bar byed pa'i dbyangs kyi yan lag rnam par dag pa bsam pa dang /bag la nyal ba dang /
58a3(phal chen): shes pa'i sa ji lta ba bzhin du rjes su 'brang ba dang /lus kyi byin gyi brlabs 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor thabs cad kyi nang du phyin kyang zil gyis mi mnon pa dang /lus kyi rgyan sgo thams cad du rnam par bstan 
58a4(phal chen): pa dang /'khor gyi dkyil 'khor thamasacad kyi sems dang /bsam pa khong du chud pas rab tu rtogs pa dang /tshig grol ba'i byin gyis brlabs kyis bstan pa dang chos 'chad pa'i byin gyi brlabs la/
58a5(phal chen): mkhas pas chos kyi gtam chen po byed de/stobs dang shugs kyi dbang thub pa med pa dang /'jigs pa med pa'i dbang du zhum pa med pa dang /chos kyi dbang zil gyis mi mnon pa dag/ye shes kyi 
58a6(phal chen): dbang gzhan gyis mi thub pa dang /shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'i dbang chos kyi mtshan nyid 'khrug pa med pa dang /tha dad pa yang dag par shes pa'i dbang mtshams sbyor ba rgyun chad pa med par bsam pa shin tu 
58a7(phal chen): rtogs pa dang /gzungs kyi dbang chos thams cad rnam par gdon mi za ba la 'jug pa dang /smon lam gyi dbang dpe sna tshogs kyis bstan pa'i tshul nye bar bsgrub pa shin tu bstan pa dang /snying rje chen po'i 
58a8(phal chen): dbang phyir mi ldog cing rgyun ma chad pa dang /byams pa chen po'i dbang sngon shin tu bskyed pas bsam pa thams cad tshim par byed pa dang /'od zer gyi dra ba chen po gtong pas chos kyis gtam chen po byed do//
58b1(phal chen): de dag ni de bzhin gshegs pa seng+ge'i gdan la bzhugs shing chos kyi gtam gsung ba dang /byang chub sems dpa' ye shes rang smon lam gyi khyad par dang ldan pa ma gtogs par sems can gyi ris na/byang chub 
58b2(phal chen): sems dpa'i dal phyir brgol ba'am/spyi gtsug blta ba'am/zil gyis gnan pa'am/dri ba'i tshogs yongs su dri bas thub par bya bar nus pa'i sems can gang yang med de/de ni gnas med do//kye rgyal ba'i sras 
58b3(phal chen): gal te bying chub sems dpa' dbang de lta bu thob pa la 'jig rten gyi khams brjod du med pa tsam du yangs pa'i 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor gyi cig yod pa 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor de yang sems can stong gsum gyi bdag po'i 
58b4(phal chen): gzi brjid thob pa sha stag gis gang ba zhig yin na yang /de dag thams cad byang chub sems dpa'i 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor du 'ongs ma thag tu lus bstan pa'i dbang gis zil gyis mnan te/byams pa chen 
58b5(phal chen): po'i dbang gis lus kyi gzhi tshim par byas nas/ye shes chen po'i dbang gis sems dang bsam pa rnam par shes par byas te/mi 'jigs pa'i bsam pa chen pos chos ston pa byed cing /spobs pa chen po'i 
58b6(phal chen): dbang gi/sems can tshim par byed do//kye rgyal ba'i sras/de ci'i phyir zhe na/'di ltar byang chub sems dpa' de ni ye shes kyi dkyil 'khor tshad med pa dang ldan pa/chos rnam par 'byed pa shin tu rnam par phye ba 
58b7(phal chen): la mkhas pa dang ldan/dran pa'i stobs dang shugs ma nyams pa rgya che ba dang ldan/chos thams cad kyi tshul rnam par gdon mi za bar bzung zhing blangs pas rtogs par mkhas pa dang ldan/byang chub sems dpa'i mkhas 
58b8(phal chen): pa mtha' dang dbus med pa dang ldan/ches kyi mchod nyid sgrib pa med pa'i chos ston pa la mkhas pa dang ldan/mos pa la mkhas pas sangs rgyas thams cad kyi byin gyis brlabs kyi ma blangs pa dang ldan/dus gsum 
59a1(phal chen): @#//gyi de bzhin gshegs pa'i dkyil 'khor du yang dag par gzhol ba'i ye shes kyi byin gyis brlabs la mkhas pa dang ldan/sems can thams cad kyi gtso bo 'das pa dang /ma byon pa dang /da 
59a2(phal chen): ltar byung ba rnams dang /rgyud kyi tshul gcig tu rgyu ba'i bsam pa bzang po rnam par dag pa la mkhas pa dang ldan pas/chos smra ba chen po byed cing /de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi chos kyi mdzod 
59a3(phal chen): kyang rnam par 'byed do//'das pa dang /ma byon dang /da ltar byung ba'i de bzhin gshegs pa rnams gyi ye shes kyi byin gyis brlab pa'i byang chub sems dpa'i yid kyi smon lam dang /ye shes 'byam 
59a4(phal chen): klas pas dam pa'i chos kyang yongs su 'dzin to//de nas byang chub sems dpa' chos kyi blo gros kyis yang byang chub sems dpa'i rim gyis yang dag par 'byung ba'i chos kyi sgo'i le'u yang dag par bstan pa'i phyir 
59a5(phal chen): sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi brlabs kyis de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//bsod nams tshogs bsags byang chub sems la gnas pa rnams//rtag tu bag yod blo brten mthu chen bdog pa dag/dran dang 
59a6(phal chen): ldan zhing byang chub sems rab mi gtod pas//phyogs rnams bcu por bde gshegs rnams ni mnyes par byed//brtson 'grus rtsoms pa dran ldan mos pa brtan pa can//'jig rten kun la rtag tu mi gnas mi 
59a7(phal chen): ldog pa//zab pa'i chos zhugs mya ngan med pa'i tshul mkhas rnams//phyogs rnams bcu po bde gshegs rnams ni mnye bar byed//brtson 'grus brten pas bde gshegs mnyes par byas nas kyang //dus gsum 
59a8(phal chen): bsod nams tshogs dang ye shes ldan pa rnams//sa rnams gnon byed spyod mchog nyams pa med par byed//thub pa'i smon lam rdzogs byed stan pa'ang de bzhin byed//ye shes gcig la zhugs pa de dag thob 
59b1(phal chen): bya ba//thob nas 'gro ba kun la sbyin pa rab tu gtong //bsam pa dag dang khams dang sbyor ba shes byas nas//dbang po'i bzhin mkhas pa de ltar chos kyang ston//pha rol phyin la brtson pa mthu chen ldan pa rnams//'gro ba rnams 
59b2(phal chen): la chos ston dag gi don mi gtong //pha rol phyin rdzogs grol ba mthu chen ldan pa rnams/khyu mchog mthu yis 'khor ba'i mtsho las sems can sgrol//de ltar nyin mtshan skyob med par brtson byed cing //drang srong rgyal ba chos 
59b3(phal chen): rigs mi gtsang zhugs pa yis//dkar po'i spyod pa gang yang rung ste spangs pa rnams//thams cad don yod rgyal ba rnams kyis sar 'gro'o//de dag ji spyod thams cad 'gro ba rnams la yang //rnam smin don yod nyon mongs 
59b4(phal chen): dri ma sbyong bar 'gyur//'di 'dra'i chos ldan de dag mun pa gsal byas shing //bdud 'khrugs 'khor lo pham byas byang chub thob par byas//de ltar brtson zhing ye shes thob pa'i bde gshegs sras//bde gshegs rnams kyi 
59b5(phal chen): chos rab mdzod kyang rab tu ston//de dag chos smra'i bya ba kun kyang shin tu byed//bdud rtsi mchog zhim sprin gyi char yang mngon par 'bebs//de dag snying rje byams pas 'gro ba kun bkang zhing //brtan pa de rnams 'khor 
59b6(phal chen): gyi bsam pa shes byas nas//gang gi bsam pa blo gros sems rgyud ci 'dra ba//de bzhin rgyal ba'i chos ni stong phrag du ma ston//glang po'i stobs sdug tshul bzang 'gro ba brtan pa rnams//seng+ge'i rtsal ltar rtag tu sus 
59b7(phal chen): kyang mi thub cing //ye shes rgya mtsho lta bu'i rab bzhin gnas pa//sprin ltar 'gro ba kun gyi nyon mongs zhi bar byed//byang chub sems dpa' chos kyi blo gros kyis de skad ces smras pa dang /de la ston pas rjes su 
59b8(phal chen): dgyes par mdzad do//chos snang ba'i le'u zhes bya ba ste/nyi shu gsum pa'o////
60a1(phal chen): @#//lho phyogs kyi 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad dang /gzhung de bzhin du steng gi phyogs kyi 'jig rten gyi kha mas kyi bang thams cad na/gling bzhi pa 'di la sogs pa gling bzhi pa re re na/de bzhin 
60a2(phal chen): gshegs pa mang po bzhugs par snang ngo//byang chub sems dpa' de dag kyang /de bzhin gshegs pa'i byin gyis brlabs kyis chos ston to//bdag kyang de bzhin gshegs pa'i byin kyis brlabs kyis chos ston te/
60a3(phal chen): bdag kyang de bzhin gshegasapa'i zhabs kyi drung na 'khod par shes so//bcom ldan 'das kyis rdzu 'phrul mngon par 'du mdzad pa ma bshig ma btang bar byang chub kyi shing drung yang ma spangs par ri rab kyi rtse mo nas/mtsho 
60a4(phal chen): ma'i gnas kyi lha'i bu rab mtshe ma'i khang pa nor bu sna tshogs gang na bder gshegs par snang ngo//ji ltar gling bzhi pa 'di na bcom ldan 'das rab mtshe ma'i gnas su gshegs par snang ba de bzhin du 'jig rten gyi khams 
60a5(phal chen): thams cad na yang /rab mtshe ma'i gnas su gshes par snang ste/byin gyis brlabs snga ma rnams kyang de bzhin mi 'khrug par snang ngo//de nas lha'i rgyal po rab mtshe mas/bcom ldan 'das rgyang ring po nas khang pa nor bu 
60a6(phal chen): sna tshogs logs su mngon par gshegs pa mthong nas/rings pa'i gzugs su khang pa nor bu sna tshogs kyi nang du//de bzhin gshegs pa'i gdan rdzu 'phrul gyis bsgrubs pa'i seng+ge'i khri padamo'i snying po can//lha rdzas kyi 
60a7(phal chen): rin po che las byas pa po gang zag brgya stong phrag bcus kun nas brgyan pa//gser gyi dra ba brgya stong phrag bcus kun gnas mngon par bres pa/me tog gi bla re brgya stong phrag bcus kun mkhyen par byas pa//phreng ba'i bla re 
60a8(phal chen): brgya stong phrag bcus kun gyi rgyas pa//spos kyi bla re brgya stong phrag bcu spos thams cad kun tu 'thul bar bdug pa//rin po che'i bla re brgya stong phrag bcu'i kun tu bres pa/me tog gi gdugs brgya stong phrag 
60b1(phal chen): lus kun nas bzung ba//phreng ba'i gdugs brgya stong phrag bcus kun nas bzung ba dang //rin po che'i gdugs brgya stong phrag bcas kun nas bzung ba dang//'od gzer brgya stong phrag bcus snang bar byas pa dang //
60b2(phal chen): rab mtshe ma'i lha'i rgyal po brgya stong phrag bcus rab tu 'dud pa/tshangs pa brgya stong phrag bcus mngon par phyag bya ba//byang chub sems dpa' brgya stong phrag bcus mngonapar bstod pa//rol mo bcud pa brgya stong phrag bcu'i sgra

60b6(phal chen): kun nas bslang ba/chos brgya stong phrag bcus rab tu brtan pa/rnam par 'phrul pa brgya stong phrag bcu dang ldan pa/yon tan brgya stong phrag bcus shin du 'phags pa/brjod pa brgya stong phrag bcum shin tu brjod pa

66b2(phal chen): dang //chos rnams la yang rmongs pa med//'das par gyur pa'i chos rnams kun//mya ngan 'das par shes gyur na//de dag shin tu mi gnas pa'i//rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas mthong ba yin//yang dag sangs rgyas sus mthong 
66b3(phal chen): zhing //dran pa shin tu bsgom byas te//mtshan med tha dad med pa rnams//de dag chos kyi rgyal po'i sras//de nas byang chub sems dpa' bo dang ldan pas/sangs rgyas kyi mthus phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te/de'i tshe 
66b4(phal chen): tshigs su bcad de smras pa//ji ltar ri mo'i las rnams la//mtshan rnams sna tshogs kun 'du ba//kun kyang 'byung ba chen por mnyam//sems kyis phongs su brtags pa yin//khams la mtshon rtsi de dag med//
66b5(phal chen): tshon rtsi la yang khams med de//khams rnams ma gtogs gzhan na yang //tshon rtsis bya ba gang yang med//sems la ri mo de med do//ri mo la yang sems med do//sems de ma gtogs gzhan du yang //ri mo 
66b6(phal chen): gang yang mi dmigs so//sems de brtags pa ma yin pa//bsam gyis mi khyab tshad med cing //phan tshun shes pa med pa yi//gzugs rnams thams cad ston par byed//ji ltar ri mo mkhan gyi rnams//sems ni gang gis 
66b7(phal chen): ri mo de//bris pa'i sems ni mi shes pa//de bzhin chos kyi rang bzhin no//sems ni ri mo mkhan dang 'dra//sems ni phung po byed pa ste//'jig rten khams ni ji snyed pa'i//'jig rten 'di dag sems kyis bris/
66b8(phal chen): sems dang 'dra ba sangs rgyas te//sangs rgyas ji bzhin 'gro ba'o//sems dang sangs rgyas dag la yang //ngo bo nyid kyis zad pa med//sems kyi rgyud rnams ci 'dra ba//de dag sangs rgyas rab tu mkhyen//de phyir sangs 
67a1(phal chen): @#//rgyas dang bzhin gyis//'gro ba sna tshogs myur bar snang //lus kyi rnams la sems med de//sems la'ang lus rnams yod pa min//sangs rgyas mdzad pa'ang rab tu byang //
67a2(phal chen): de ltar spyod pa ngo mtshar che//gang zhig dus gsum thams cad kyi//rgyal ba thams cad shes 'dod na//sangs rgyas thams cad sems kyi dngos//chos kyi dbyings su blta bar gyis//de nas byang 
67a3(phal chen): chub sems dpa' ye shes dang ldan pas/sangs rgyas kyi mthus phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad de smras pa//gzung du med la 'dzin ces bya//snang ba med la snang ba ste//mnyan 
67a4(phal chen): du med par nyan pa med//sems med pa yis sems dang ldan//tshad med pa la tshad ma yis//gang zhig 'dzin par mi 'dod pa//de ni tshad med tshad ma yin//nam yang 'dzin par mi 'gyur ro//brjod du 
67a5(phal chen): med pa sgra byed pa//bdag la shin tu bslu ba ste//de yi don kyang rdzogs mi byed//srog chags rnams kyang tshim par 'gyur//de bzhin mtha' yas brjod ldan pa'i//brjod pa gang zhig smra 'dod pa//
67a6(phal chen): bskal ba grangs med zad byas kyang //rang 'byung rnams kyis brjod mi 'du//yid bzhin nor bu dag pa ni//kha dog kun tu rab tu snang //de la kha dog yod pa min//sangs rgyas yon tan de bzhin no//ji ltar nam mkha' dri 
67a7(phal chen): med cing //gzugs med bstan du med yin yang //gzugs rnams thams cad ston par byed//nam mkha' snang ba gang yang med//de bzhin thams cad gzigs pa rnams//yon tan kun dang ldan ston 
67a8(phal chen): kyang //de ni sus kyang mthong ba med//sems la'ang de ni mi dmigs so//sangs rgyas sgra ni grag mod kyi//de bzhin gshegs pa sgra ma yin//sgra ni ma gtogs gzhan du yang //sangs rgyas shes par mi rung ngo //
67b1(phal chen): ji ltar bdag gi mthong tshul du//'di ni shes bya'i gnas ma yin//sangs rgyas byang chub blang du med//de la rnam par rtog pa med//sangs rgyas chos rnams gang dag kyang //su la'ang sangs rgyas gsung ba med//
67b2(phal chen): sems can bsam pa ji bzhin du//de la de 'dra ston pa mdzad//shin tu rgyas pa chen po'i mdo las sangs rgyas phal po che zhes bya ba las/rab mtshe ma'i gnas su byang chub sems dpa' 'dus pas/
67b3(phal chen): tshigs su bcad pa bstan pa'i le'u zhes bya ba ste/nyi shu lnga pa////sangs rgyas rmad ga gcad ces bya ba shin tu rgyas pa chen po'i mod//bam po drug pa////de nas de'i tshe byang chub sems dpa' 
67b4(phal chen): man tan dang ldan pa/sangs rgyas kyi mthus/byang chub sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin shin tu rnam par phye ba'i rnam par 'byed pa zhes bya ba la mnyam par bzhag par gyur to//de ltar byang 
67b5(phal chen): chub sems dpa' yon tan dang ldan pa byang chub sems dpa'i ting de 'dzin rnam par phye ba rnam par 'byed pa de la mnyam par bzhag ma thag tu/de'i tshe phyogs bcu re rer yang /'jig rten 
67b6(phal chen): gyi khams sangs rgyas kyi zhing stong phrag bcu'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed 'das pa'i pha rol nas/de bzhin gshegs pa'i mtshan yon tan dang ldan pa zhes bya ba/sangs rgyas kyi zhing stong prag bcu'i 
67b7(phal chen): rdul shin tu phra ba snyed kyis zhal bstan pa mthong ste/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das de dag gis de la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to//yon tan dang ldan pa de ltar khyod byang chub sems dpa'i ting? nge 'dzin shin tu 
67b8(phal chen): rnam par phye ba'i rnam pa 'byed pa la mnyam par bzhag pa legs so legs si//rigs kyi bu khyod la ni de bzhin gshegs pa sangs rgyas kyi zhing stong brag bcu rdul shin tu phra ba snyed? thams cad kyang mchod 
68a1(phal chen): @#//'thun pa sha stag gis/'di ltar bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa rnam par snang mdzad kyi sngon gyi smon lam dang sngon gyi zil byin gyis brlabs dang /byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi 
68a2(phal chen): dge ba'i rtsa bas shin tu bstan pas na 'di ltar chos bstan pa dang /sangs rgyas kyi ye shes rnam par 'phel bar bya ba dang /'jig rten gyi khams la 'jug par bya ba dang /sems can gyi khams rnam par sgom pa dang /
68a3(phal chen): chags pa med pa la 'jug par bya ba dang |sgrib pa med pa'i spyod yul dang /kun nas rab tu sbyor ba dang /thams cad mkhyen pa la rab tu bsgrub pa dang /chos thams cad khong du chud par bya ba dang /dbang po thams 
68a4(phal chen): cad yongs su shes par bya ba dang /chos bstan pa thams cad yongs su gzung bar bya ba dang /'di ltar byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa bcu brtsom pa rab tu 'thob par bya ba'i phyir/byin gyis brlabs pa

68a6(phal chen): byang chub sems dpa' yon tan dang ldan pa la chags pa med pa nyid nye bar bsgrub pa mdzad do//bsgrubs pa med pa nyid nye bar sgrub pa mdzad do//byin gyis brlabs pa nyid nye bar bsgrub par mdzad do//
68a7(phal chen): slob dpon med pa nyid nye bar bsgrubs pa mdzad do//rmongs pa med pa nyid nye bar pa bsgrub pa mdzad do//tha dad pa med pa nyid nye bar 'grub pa mdzad do//kha na ma tho ba med pa nyid nye bar bsgru ba mdzad do//tshad 
68a8(phal chen): med pa nyid nye bar bsgrub ba mdzad do//zil gyis mi non pa nyid nye bar bsgrub pa badzad do//zhum pa med pa nyid nye bar ba sgrub pa mdzad do//de ci'i phyir zhe na/ting nge 'dzin 'di nyid kyi chos nyid thob pa'i 
68b1(phal chen): phyir ro//de nas sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das de dag gis/phyag g.yas ba brkyang ste/byang chub sems dpa' yon tan dang ldan pa'i mgo la byug pa mdzad do//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasade dag gis de ltar 
68b2(phal chen): reg ma thag tu/byang chub sems dpa' mon tan dang ldan pa/ting nge 'dzin de las langs te/byang chub sems dpa' rnams la smras pa/kye rgyal ba'i sras dag/byang chub sems dpa'i las ni bsam gyis mi khyab 
68b3(phal chen): ste/de ni 'di ltar chos kyi dbyings kyis klas s+ho//nam mkha'i dbyings kyi mthar thug go/kye rgyal ba'i sras dang /de ci'i phyir zhe na/de ni 'di ltar byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams ni/'das pa'i sangs 
68b4(phal chen): rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi spyod pa la yang slob po//ma byon pa'i sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'da' rnams kyi spyod pa la yang sleb po//de ltar byung ba'i sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'a rnams kyi spyod pa la yang
68b5(phal chen): po/kye rgyal ba'i sras dang/byang chub sems dpa' rna mas kyi spyod pa gang la bsam te/de skad du smras pa de dag gang zhe na/kye rgyal ba'i sras dag/bcu po 'di dag ni byang chub sems dpa' rnam sa kyi spyod pa/sangs rgyas 
68b6(phal chen): bcom ldan 'das/'das pa dang/ma byon pa dang /da ltar byung ba rnams kyis gsungs pa dang/gsung ba dang /gsung bar 'gyur ba ste/bcu gang zhe na 'di lta ste dga' bar byed pa zhes bya ba/byang chub sems dpa'i spyod 
68b7(phal chen): pa dang po dang /bde ba bskyed pa zhes bya ba byang chub sems dpa'i spyodapa gnyis pa dang/mi mthun pa mad ba zhes bya ba byang chub sem dpa'i spyod pa gsum pa dang/mi zad pa zhes bya ba byang chub sams dpa'i

68b9(phal chen): sems 
69a1(phal chen): @#//dpa'i spyod pa bdun pa dang /thub par dka' ba zhes bya ba byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa brgyad pa dang /chos bzang po zhes bya ba byang chub sems/dpa'i spyod pa dgu pa dang /
69a2(phal chen): bdun pa dang ldan pa zhes bya ba byang chub semasad pa'i spyod pa bcu pa dag ste/kye rgyal ba'i sras dag/bcu po de dag ni byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi spyod pa/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das/
69a3(phal chen): 'das pa dang /ma byon pa dang /da ltar byung ba rnams kyis gsungs pa dang /gsung pa dang /gsung bar 'gyur ba'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras dag/de la byang chub sems dpa'i spyodapa dang po

69a8(phal chen): rol la phan gdags pa dang /sems can thams cad la/phan gdags pa'i sems kyis/sbyin pa'i bdag po sbyin pa byed pa dang /kye rgyal ba'i sras dag/'di ni byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa dang /
69b1(phal chen): po'o//spyod pa de la gnas pa'i byang chub sems dpa' ni/skye bo mang po'i yid du 'ong ba yin te/de ni sems can dbul po gang dang gang na yod pa de dang der smon lam gyi dbang gis rab tu skyes ste/skyes 
69b2(phal chen): nas kyang zad mi shes pa'i yongs spyod dang ldan zhing shin tu phyug pa yin te/de la gal te dus gcig tu/slong ba'i srog chags bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong grangs med pa zhig 'ongs te/'di skad du shes par 
69b3(phal chen): gyis shig/bdag cag thams cad ni bkres pas mchi'o//gal te bdag cag khyod kyi sha zos na ni 'tsho bar 'gyur ro zhes/tshig tu smra bar gyur kyang /byang chub sems dpa' ni sbyin pa phun sum 
69b4(phal chen): tshogs pa rnam pa 'di lta bu dang ldan pa/slong ba'i srog chags bya ba khrag khrig brgya stong grags med pa de snyed cig nye bar lhags kyang /byang chub sems dba' la da'i phyir sems bskyed pa gcig kyang zhum 
69b5(phal chen): pa'i bsam pa mi 'gyur gyi/sems can de dag thams cad la snying rje chen por rab tu skye ste/gzhan mang po zhig 'ongs kyang de bas ches dga' ba dang /spro ba 'byung zhing 'di ltar bdag gis gsol ba ma btab 
69b6(phal chen): par/sangs rgyas kyi chos la yang dag par spobs pa'i dge ba'i bshes gnyen sdug bsngal zhing slong ba sbyin pa'i gnas su gyur pa 'di lta bu dag dang 'grogs par gyur pa ni/bdag gis thob pa legs 
69b7(phal chen): par thob ste/ji nas kyang sems can 'ga'i yid kyang ma nyi mas par bya ba de ltar bdag gis bya'o//bdag gis dge ba'i rtsa ba ce byas pa dang /ci byed pa dang /dge ba'i rtsa ba ci byed par 'gyur ba des 
69b8(phal chen): ji tsam gyis sems can de dag thams can 'grangs shing dus byed par 'gyur ba de tsam du che ba'i lus su gyur cig/sems can de dag 'grangs shing tshim par ma gyur gyi bar du gcad cing skye bas mi zad 
70a1(phal chen): @#//pa de tsam gyi lus su gyur cig/dge ba'i rtsa ba des kyang bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu yang dag par bsgrubs te/phung po ma lus pa'i mya ngan las 'das 
70a2(phal chen): pa'i khams su mya ngan las 'das par bya'o//rnam grangs 'di bzhin du ma 'ongs pa'i dus kyi 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad kyi sems can de dag thams cad las 'di 'dra ba dang ldan par

70a4(phal chen): kyis sangs rgyas kyi mdzad pa byas nas/phung po'i lhag ma ma lus pa'i mya ngan las las 'das pa'i dbyings su mya ngan las 'da' bar gyur cig/bdag nyid kyang sems can gyi bsam pa yongs 
70a5(phal chen): su rdzogs par ma gyur gyi bar du/bdag du 'du shes pa'am/sems can du 'du shes pa'am/srid par 'du shes pa'am/srog tu 'du shes pa'am/so so'i skye bor 'du shes pa'am/

70a7(phal chen): nye bar bzung ba dang /sems can gyi khams mtha' yas pa'i chos nyid nye bar bzung ba dang /stong pa'i chos nyid nye bar bzun pa dang /'dod chags dang bral ba'i chos nyid nye bar

70b8(phal chen): snyam du sems ste/kye rgyal ba'i sras dag 'di ni byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa dang pos/dga' bar byed pa zhes bya'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras dag/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi spyod pa gnyis pa bde ba

71a2(phal chen): pa ston pa yin/rigs kyi bdag nyid che ba la mi gnas pa yin/rgyud kyi bdag nyid che ba la mig gnas pa yin/zas kyi bdag nyid che ba las mi gnas pa yin/gzugs kyi bdag nyid che ba ba la mi gnas pa ni 
71a3(phal chen): longs spyod kyi bdag nyid che ba la ma gnas pa yin te/de ltar de ni tshul khrims las yongs su nyams pa med cing /'di ltar kun nas 'khor ba med pa dang /kun tu btsal ba med pa dang /yongs su gdung 
71a4(phal chen): ba med pa dang /bar chad med pa dang /gtso ba med pa dang /'khrugs pa med par bya'o//snang bar med par bya'o//sangs rgyas kyi sngags pa'i chos rnams yongs su bzung snyam du sems te/kye rgyal ba'i sras dag byang chub 
71a5(phal chen): sems dpa' de lta bu'i tshul khrims dang ldan pa'i drung du/gal te nyin gcig tu bdud bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong zhig 'ongs te/bdud re re zhing yang lha'i bu mo bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong 'dod pa'i cho ga thams cad

71b4(phal chen): nas/bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu mngon par 'tshang rgya bar byas te/phung po lhag ma ma lus pa'i mya ngan las 'das pa'i dbyings su yongs su mya ngan las 'da' bar 'gyur ba de ltar bya'o//de ci'i 
71b5(phal chen): phyir zhe na/'di ltar sangs rgyas thams cad kyi rjes su bslabs te/mi bya ba thams cad yongs su spang pa dang /lus kyis mi shes pa thams cad yongs su spangs pa ma yin pa dang /sems kyis kyang ma yin pa da ni 
71b6(phal chen): bdag cag gi las yin gyis/kye rgyal ba'i sras bdag cag gis sangs rgyas kyi chos thams cad kyi rjes su song ba'i ye shes kyis sems can thams cad kyi phyin ci log rab tu spang pa'i phyir chos bstan par 
71b7(phal chen): bya'o//phyin ci log sems can rnams las gzhan 'ang med de/phyin ci log rnams las kyang sems can rnams gud na med do//phyin ci log tu yang sems can 'dus pa ma yin/sems can du yang phyin ci log

135b3(phal chen): ye shes kyi stobs rdo rje'i rgyal mtshan gyi mthu'i mchog thob pa dang /rdo rje gcod pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyi tshul rab tu thob pa dang /sems can bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong grangs med pa rnams yongs su 
135b4(phal chen): smin par gdul ba la rmongs pa med pa dang /sangs rgyas kyi rnam par 'phrul ba thams cad du grob gdon mi za ba'i mchog thob pa dang /bgrang ba thams cad la mngon par chags pa dang /bgrang ba'i mngon 
135b5(phal chen): par bsgrub pa/thams cad shin tu bslabs pa dang /bgrang ba'i shes pa thams cad kyi mthar thug cing chos rnam par dgod pa la mkhas pa/'di dag dang gzhan yang yon tan bye ba khrag khrig brgya 
135b6(phal chen): stong ba rjod du mod pa dang /yon tan sngags pa rnam par dag pa mi zad pas byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi dus gsum gyi yon tan tshad med pa dang ldan pa sha stag go/de bzhin gshegs pa 
135b7(phal chen): mngon sum du bya ba dang /sangs rgyas 'byam klas pa thams cad blta ba'i phyir kun nas 'dus par gyur to//de nas byang chub sems dpa' rdo rje rgyal mtshan gyis/sangs rgyas kyi byin gyis rlabs 
135b8(phal chen): kyis phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di skad smras so//yang dag sangs rgyas skye ba med//rgyal ba mya ngan mi 'da' ste//rgyu mthun pa yi smon lam gyis/
136a1(phal chen): @#//des ni rnam par 'phrul ba snang //'di dag chos ni bsam mi khyab//'di dag sems ni spyod yul min//gang gis ye shes pha rol phyin//des ni sangs rgyas 
136a2(phal chen): spyod yul mthong //lus 'di yang dag sangs rgyas min//sgra yang de bzhin gshegs pa min//lus dang sgra las gzhan na yang //sangs rgyas rnam par 'phrul mi mthong //sngon gyi las 
136a3(phal chen): rnams ma gtogs par//rang byung rnams kyi spyod yul gyi//mchog gi tshul rnams shes pa ni//blo zhan rnams kyis mi nus so//yang dag sangs rgyas rnam 'phrul 'di//gang dag nas 
136a4(phal chen): kyang ma 'ongs ste//gang du 'gro bar mi 'gyur ro//'di dag rang byung sku yin no//tshad med shin tu 'dren pa rnams//chos 'di rab tu bshad mdzad kyang //chos la gnas pa gang yang med//
136a5(phal chen): zhing rnams bye ba kun na snang //mkhyen pas mtha' dang dbus med de//chos kun thugs su chud cing rtogs//chos kyi dbyings la rab zhugs nas//rnam par 'phrul pa ston pa mdzad//chos 
136a6(phal chen): rnams kun la chags pa med//'gro la de ni bsgribs mi mnga'//tshad med pa yi yon tan gyis//zhing rnams kun na rab tu snang //gang zhig ye shes 'di dag dang //ye shes thams cad 
136a7(phal chen): rtogs 'dod pas//bsam pa rnam par sbyang byas ste//byang chub spyod pa spyad par bya//drang srong chen pos byin gyis rlabs//'di 'dra gang gis mthong gyur cing //da ni the tshom mi byed na//dge 
136a8(phal chen): bshes mchog la bsnyen pa yin//de nas byang chub sems dpa' dam pa'i rgyal mtshan gyis//sangs rgyas kyi byin gyis rlabs kyis phyogs bcur rnam par bltas nas//de'i tshe tshigs su 
136b1(phal chen): bcad pa 'di dag smas so//shin tu zab ste brjod du med//brjod du yod kyang 'da' bar dka'//tshig gi lam las shin tu 'das//nam mkha' la ni rgyal ba snang //de bzhin gshegs pa'i byin 
136b2(phal chen): rlabs kyis//mi'i seng ge mthong bar 'gyur//yang dag sangs rgyas rtog med kyang //rgyal ba rnam par rtog par snang //'jig rten mgon pos chos nyid dngos//zab mo 'di dag yang dag bsnan//
136b3(phal chen): gang zag mtshungs pa med pa yi//des ni sku 'di rab tu 'byung //thams cad gzigs kyi spyod yul gyi//ye shes chen po 'di 'dra ste//'di las gang zhig rtogs 'dod pa//des ni rgyal ba 
136b4(phal chen): mngon du byos//yid kyi dbang po rnam dag pas//de bzhin gshegs pa bsnyen bkur zhing //'di yi tshul las 'da' byed de//nam yang skyo bar mi bya'o//mi zad pa yi bsod nams dang //byang chub 
136b5(phal chen): sems dpa' bstan pa rnams//the tsom rnam par bzlog byas te//de dag la ni skyo med bltos//rgyal sras gang gis chos dbyings kyi//shin tu bstan pa'i mthar phyin pa//des ni kun 
136b6(phal chen): gzigs rnam 'phrul pa//'di dag shin tu 'da' bar bya//dad pa chos 'di'i rtsa ba zhes//chos kyi phung pos bshad mdzad kyis//dad pa shin tu cher bskyed la//byang chub spyod pa spyad par 
136b7(phal chen): gyis//sangs rgyas sti stang gang byed cing //de bzhin gshegs la drin gzo ba//yang dag sangs rgyas dpal ldan pas//de dag rgyang ma ring gnas so//bdag nyid che ba 'di lta bu//gang gis 
136b8(phal chen): thos mthong mkhas pa rnams//spyod yul thob par cis 'gyur ba'i//smon lam su zhig sbyor mi byed//de nas byang chub sems dpa' dpa' ba'i rgyal mtshan gyis sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi rlabs kyis phyogs 
137a1(phal chen): @#//bcud rnam par bltas nas/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//ji ltar nyi ma'i 'od snang bas//mig gis gzugs rnams mthong gyur pa//de bzhin 
137a2(phal chen): sangs rgyas byin rlabs kyis//dad pa'i sems ldan rgyal ba mthong //ji ltar brtson 'grus stobs ldan pa//rgya mtsho la ni 'jug par byed//de bzhin ye shes stobs ldan pas//
137a3(phal chen): bsam du med pa'i sangs rgyas mthong //ji ltar sa bon rnam dag pa//zhing bzang la ni skyer 'gyur ba//de bzhin sems bzang rnam dag la//sangs rgyas chos ni skye bar 'gyur//ji ltar 
137a4(phal chen): nor gyi phung po yis//dbul ba thams cad spong byed pa//de bzhin sangs rgyas chos rnams kyis//byang chub sems dpa' rnam dag 'byung //ji ltar dug du gyur pa la//nad med dug sman rgyal bar 
137a5(phal chen): byed//de bzhin nyon mongs rnam pa la//rnam dag tshul ni spyod yul lo//sangs rgyas kyis ni gang bsngags pa'i//dge ba'i bshes la brten byas na//de yi mthus ni sangs rgyas kyi//chos nyid 'di 
137a6(phal chen): dag thos bar 'gyur//skal pa bye ba grangs med pas//sbyin pa'i mchog rnams sbyin byas kyang //'di yi tshul la rab spyod pa'i//grangs dang char yang mi 'gro'o//sangs rgyas yon tan mtha' 
137a7(phal chen): yas te//mtshan gyis shin tu rnam par bris//brjod pas yang dag sangs rgyas rnams//rgyal ba shes par mi nus so//rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rkang gnyis mchog/gnas nas g.yo ba mi 
137a8(phal chen): mnga' bar//sku ni kun tu rnam rgyu zhing //chos kyi dbyings ni ston pa mdzad//ji ltar nam mkha' khams 'di ni//nam yang rang bzhin mi 'gyur te//de bzhin sangs rgyas chos nyid kyang //ngo bo nyid las 
137b1(phal chen): 'gyur ba med//de nas byang chub sems dpa' skar ma'i rgyal mtshan gyis/sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi rlabs kyis/phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di smras so//mi yi 'jig rten 
137b2(phal chen): 'di kun dang //lha yi gnas rnams thams cad dang //'gro ba ma lus kun na yang //mi yi gtso bo shin tu snang //ji ltar sems dngos gcig bu las//rnam pa tha dad sems brjod pa//de bzhin sangs 
137b3(phal chen): rgyas sku gcig las//sangs rgyas thams cad brjod pa yin//byang chub gnyis su med pa brjod//byang chub de yang mtshan nyid med//mtshan gyis mngon par rnam bris pa'i//sku rnams gnyis su snang 
137b4(phal chen): bar gyur//mkha' dang chos kyi dbyings la zhugs//sgyu ma'i sa yang rab tu bstan//de phyir sangs rgyas de dag gi/spyod yul blta ba zad pa med//yang dag sangs rgyas chos kyi sku//mgon po thams 
137b5(phal chen): cad dus gsum du//ji ltar 'dul ba'i 'gro ba la//gzugs kyi sku yang rab tu snang //'di 'dra'i lus su bdag gyur ces//sangs rgyas rtog par mi mdzad de//ston la rnam rtog mi mnga' yang //byin gyi 
137b6(phal chen): rlabs ni ston pa mdzad//dbyer med pa yi dbyings la yang //chos kyi dbyings ni yang dag gnas//'jig rten khams ni tshad med par//thub pas rnam par rtog pa bstan//sku ni sprul pa ma yin te//
137b7(phal chen): sprul pa min pa'ang sku ma yin//sprul pa med pa'i chos 'di yis//sprul pa rnams ni rab tu bstan//yang dag sangs rgyas tshad med pa//chos kyi dbyings la shin tu brten//mtha' dad dbus med rab tu 
137b8(phal chen): zab//tshig gi lam gyis bzla bar dka'//kun tu 'gro ba'i chos rnams la//de bzhin gshegs pa don des mkhyen//zhing rnams kun la chags mi mnga'//chos kyi dbyings la rnam par spyod//
138a1(phal chen): @#//de nas byang chub sems dpa' ye shes rgyal mtshan gyis sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi brlabs kyis phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras 
138a2(phal chen): so//kun mkhyen ye shes ma chags pa//de la zhugs par gang gyur pa//de yi sems ni tshad mi zin//byang chub kyi ni spyad pa spyod//de bzhin gshegs pa mang po rnams//yul dang yul 
138a3(phal chen): du de dag snang //chos rnams phyogs gcig gang la yang //yang dag sangs rgyas gnas pa med//'jig rten sgron me de dag gis//byin gyi rlabs ni gang bstan pa//bskal pa bsam gyis 
138a4(phal chen): mi khyab par//'di yi mtha' ni rnyed par dka'//dus gsum rnams ni ji snyed pa'i//sems can rnams kyi mthar thug pa//bgrang ba'i mthar mi phyin par nus//'dren pa la ni mthar thug med//
138a5(phal chen): de dag sku ni gcig tu snang //sku gnyis de dag bsam mi khyab//'jig rten khams su snang gyur kyang //sku la gnyis su gyur pa med//ji ltar zla ba shar gyur pa//tshad med pa yi gzugs brnyan 
138a6(phal chen): rnams//chu yi snod du snang gyur kyang //zla ba gnyis su gyur pa med/de bzhin chags med mkhyen ldan rnams//chags med rnam par 'tshang rgya ba//zhing rnams kun la rab tu snang //sangs 
138a7(phal chen): rgyas la ni gnyis kyang med//'di la gcig kyang yod ma yin//sku ni gnyis sam gsum du med//ji ltar sems can bsam pa bzhin//tshad med par ni rab tu snang //sku ni 'das pa ma yin te/
138a8(phal chen): ma 'ongs par ni sku ma yin//skyes dang byang chub mya ngan 'das//skad cig gis ni rab tu snang //ji ltar sgyu mar gyur pa'i gzugs//skyes pa ma yin 'ongs pa min//de ltar de bzhin 
138b1(phal chen): gshegs pa'i sku//skye ba med par shin tu bstan/de nas byang chub sems dpa' tshan po che'i rgyal mtshan gyis/sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi rlabs kyis phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te//de'i tshe tshigs

138b3(phal chen): par brjod//sangs rgyas yul ni ma lus khyab//bar snang nam mkha' ji lta bar//de bzhin sangs rgyas bsam mi khyab//der ni sems la 'pho ba med//sems la skye ba'ang yod ma yin//sangs rgyas 
138b4(phal chen): rnams kyi spyod yul ni//skye ba med la rab tu gnas//ji ltar rab rib ldan pa'i mig/nang na med cing phyi na'ang med//de bzhin 'jig rten sangs rgyas dag/nang na mi bzhugs phyi na'ang min//'jig rten 
138b5(phal chen): kun gyi don gyi phyir//rkang gnyis kyi ni mchog byed de//'jig rten du mar byung ba yang //'jig rten dag tu skye bos mthong //sangs rgyas rnams la zhing mi gnas//rgyal ba dag la nyin mtshan med/

138b7(phal chen): bskal pa yod ma yin//dus kyang ma lus med pa ste//dus gsum yang dag rdzogs sangs rgyas//mgon po rnams ni mnga' bar 'gyur//ji ltar nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor ni//nams kyang mtshan dang 
138b8(phal chen): mi gnas te//de bzhin mang na rgya mtshos nyid do//bskal pa gcig dang dus gsum du//'dren par bzhugs pa ma yin te//dus gsum zhes kyang rab tu brjod//sangs rgyas rnams kyi chos 
139a1(phal chen): @#//nyid ltos//de nas byang chub sems dpa' rdul dang bral ba'i rgyal mtshan gyis/sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi rlabs kyis phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 
139a2(phal chen): 'di dag smras so//yang dag sangs rgyas sku cig ste//'dren pa thams cad don gcig kyang //tshad med gzhan dang gzhan dag du//phyogs bcu kun na rab tu snang //gang gis ma lus thams cad kyi//chos 
139a3(phal chen): kyi dbyings rnams kun khyab pas//sems can gtso bo thub pa yin//spyod yul rnams ni ci 'dra ltos//sangs rgyas rnams kyi chos nyid ni//nang gi skus kyang snang ba min//de bzhin gshegs pa'i 
139a4(phal chen): byin gyi brlabs//phyi rol skus kyang mi snang ngo//dus rnams kun tu las rnams kyis//ji ltar las rnams bsags pa bzhin//de bzhin so sor phye ba'i skus//rigs su byung bar rab tu bstan//
139a5(phal chen): rgyal ba'i chos nyid ma gtogs par//sangs rgyas sku ni ci 'dra ba//grangs su bgrang bar mi nus te//bsam pas lta yang shes mi nus//ji ltar bdag cag sems kyi las//bsam gyis mi khyab 
139a6(phal chen): mi 'byung ba//de bzhin sangs rgyas bsam mi khyab//sems kyi 'phrin las mi mnga'o//ji ltar zhing rnams bsam yas pa//sa la rnam par dag par mdzes//de bzhin sangs rgyas ba sam mi khyab//'go 
139a7(phal chen): ba dag na mdzes par snang //ji ltar las rnams 'jug pa ni//rkyen gyis rab tu 'byung 'gyur ba//de bzhin rgyu rnams 'thun pa las//sangs rgyas shin tu mthong bar 'gyur//yid bzhin nor bu'i rgyal po 
139a8(phal chen): ni//ji lta yid bsam rdzogs byed pa//de bzhin sangs rgyas chos nyid kyang //rtag tu smon lam rdzogs par bsgrub//mtha' dang dbus med 'jig rten 'dir//'jig rten mgon po 'dren par 'gyur//de yi 
139b1(phal chen): smon lam de dag gis//phyogs bcu 'di dag rgyal bas gang //de nas byang chub sems dpa' dri ma med pa'i rgyal mtshan gyis/sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi brlabs kyis/phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te//

139b3(phal chen): des ste sems can mnyam sangs rgyas//sems can la la bsam byas na//rgyal ba de dag thams cad mthong //sangs rgyas la ni gshegs pa med//sangs rgyas la ni dmigs byas nas//sems kyis 
139b4(phal chen): sangs rgyas dmigs pas na//sems bskyed pa dang rgyal mnyam par//de bzhin de dag rgyal ba mthong //dkar po chos kyis nyer brtan cing //gang gis grags pa rnam sbyangs pa//de dag thams cad 
139b5(phal chen): sems bskyed la//shes rab ldan pa rab tu bzhugs//dmigs pa dag dang byin gyis brlabs//mi yi gtso bo ston pa mdzad//de'i phyir dmigs pa kun tu yang //sangs rgyas rgyal ba gshegs 
139b6(phal chen): par brjod//'jig rten khams rnams 'di dag ni//sangs rgyas sku rnams bdag gi med//sems la bdag gi med pa bstan//byang chub rtogs la bdag gi med//mi yi seng ge'i chos nyid dang //
139b7(phal chen): dpa' bo stobs la rab tu gnas//lus dang mtshan nyid mi 'dra ste//snang ba'i sems dang mnyam pa'i sku//ji ltar 'jig rten de bzhin sku//ji bzhin skur yang rgyal ba ste//sku yi rang bzhin shes pa 
139b8(phal chen): ni//sangs rgyas chags pa med ces bya//sangs rgyas kun tu gzigs pa ste//chos rnams thams cad rab tu gzigs//sangs rgyas chos dang byang chub rnams//gang la'ang dmigs pa med pa yin//
140a1(phal chen): @#//sangs rgyas la ni gshegs pa med//sangs rgyas gang na'ang mi bzhugs ste//yang dag sangs rgyas log pa med//de bzhin gshegs pa len mi mnga'//de nas byang

140a4(phal chen): dang //bsam pa bzhin du mya ngan 'da'//sangs rgyas la ni su zhig sems//ci phyir sangs rgyas bsam mi khyab//yang dag sangs rgyas su yis mthong //rgyal ba sus kyang mthong mi 'gyur//
140a5(phal chen): ji ltar chos rnams chos nyid dang //ji ltar sangs rgyas spyod yul du//gang dag chos rnams gcig tsam yang //de bzhin nyid la mi 'jug go/'di ni 'jig rten 'di rgyal zhes//'jig rten dag na rnam 
140a6(phal chen): rtog yod//gang gis ngo bo nyid shes na//'jig rten med de rgyal ba med//'jig rten dad par bya ba'i phyir//mgon po dag ni mngon sum ston//de dag gi ni lta ba 'di//shin tu dmigs par mi 
140a7(phal chen): rung de//gang gis chags pa rnam bzlog cing //chags pa med pa dga' byed pa//chags pa thams cad rnam bzlog nas//chags med chos nyid rtogs par 'gyur//sangs rgyas zhes brjod gang yin 
140a8(phal chen): dang //gang gi rnam 'phrul snang gyur pa'i//chos ni dus gsum sems can du//rnam pa kun tu mi rnyed do//gang gis chos nyid 'di shes shing //gang gis 'di bzhin yid shes na//thams cad mkhyen cing gzigs kun pa'i//de bzhin 
140b1(phal chen): gshegs pa myur du 'gyur//sangs rgyas thams cad rnam 'phrul ba//'di dag thams cad tshig gis bstan//yang dag sangs rgyas tshig la ma med//tshig gi lam gyis rab tu brjod//de nas byang chub sems dpa' chos kyi 
140b2(phal chen): rgyal mtshan gyis/sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi brlabs kyis phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te/de'i tshe chags su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//gang gis rnam par 'phrul snang ba'i//sangs rgyas mthong ba med pa bas//'jig rten 
140b3(phal chen): khams rnams thams cad ni//sdug bsngal phung po blangs kyang bla//'gro ba gang gis byang chub du//sems ni nam yang ma bskyed pa//de dag gnas 'di thos gyur na//sangs rgyas byang chub 'grub par 'gyur//gang gis 
140b4(phal chen): bsam pa gcig gis kyang //'di la mos byed mkhas pa ni//mi yi mchog du mi 'gyur zhes//des ni the tsom mi bya'o//de yi rnam par 'phrul pa ni//bskal pa bye ba'i bar du bkang //su zhig 'di la mos byed pa//de dag 
140b5(phal chen): bla na med par 'gyur//brjod du med pa'i sangs rgyas rnams//skad cig skad cig bsnyen bkur kyang //'di li tshul la ma gnas na//de dag bsnyen bkur byas ma yin//gang las sangs rgyas brjod 'gyur ba'i//chos 
140b6(phal chen): 'di gang gis thos gyur pa//des ni mi bde kun gyis kyang //spyad pa rnams ni blang bar bya//ji tsam chos kyi dbyings rnams dang //dus gsum gyi ni mgon po yi//sangs rgyas rnams ni brton pa yi//ye shes chen po 
140b7(phal chen): gcig thos nas//phyi ma'i skal ba bye ba rnams//zhing rnams re rer zad byas shing //'di yi tshul ni brtson byed na//de las byang chub dgon pa med//sems can rnams ni sngon mtha' yi//'khor bar mi rnams 
140b8(phal chen): gnas gyur kyang //des ni sangs rgyas rab byung ba//chos nyid 'di nyid shes ma gyur//chos rnams zhig pa byas pa med//chos ni 'jig par mi 'gyur te//kun tu snang bas rnam 'phrul ba//'jig rten dag du 
141a1(phal chen): thams cad bstan//dga' ldan gyi gnas su byang chub sems dpa' 'dus pa'i tshigs su bcad pa bstan pa'i le'u ste//nyi shu dgu pa'o//sangs rgyas rma dag cad ces bya ba shan tu 
141a2(phal chen): rgyas pa chen po'i mdo////bam po bcu cig pa////de nas byang chub sems dpa' rdo rje'i rgyal mtshan de'i tshe sangs rgyas kyi mthus/ting nge 'dzin ye shes snang ba zhes bya ba la snyoms par zhugs te//byang chub 
141a3(phal chen): sems dpa' rdo rje'i rgyal mtshon byang chub sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin ye shes snang pa zhes bya ba de la snyoms par zhugs ma thag tu/de'i tshe na phyogs bcu'i phyogs re rer 'jig rten gyi khams sangs rgyas kyi zhing 
141a4(phal chen): stong gi rdul shin tu phra ba snyed 'das pa'i phra rol nas/de bzhin gshegs pa sangs rgyas kyi zhing brgya phrag stong gi rdul shin tu phra pa snyed kyis zhal bstan pa mthong ste/de dag kyang 'di lta ste/mtshan rdo rje'i rgyal mtshan

203b6(phal chen): dril bu'i dra ba kun nas spyangs pa'i sgra yid du 'ong zhing snyan pa 'byung ba'i gdugs dang /ye shes sgrib pa med cing chags pa med pa'i gdugs dang /sangs rgyas thams cad la mngon par dgab pa'i 
203b7(phal chen): gdugs yongs su btang ba'i phyir yongs su bsngo'o//de ltar byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po gdugs kyi sbyin pas yang dag par bzung ba'i dge ba'i rtsa ba de dag sems can    
203b8(phal chen): thams cad la ye shes mchog gi gdugs kyis mngon par dgab ba'i phyir yongs su bsngo'o/sangs rgyas thams cad kyi yon tan gyis kun nas g.yogs shing rab tu brgyan pa'i phyir 
204a1(phal chen): @#//yongs su bsngo'o//sangs rgyas kyi ye shes dang yon tan dgongs pa dam pa rnam par dag par bya ba'i phyir yongs su bsngo'o//sems rin po che mtha' yas pa la dbang

284a5(phal chen): g.yo shin tu brtan thob pa//ye shes stobs ldan tshul la mkhas gyur pa//de dag rgyal ba'i mdzod kyi tshul la zhugs//de dag tshul gyi rgyal po'i tshul shes 
284a6(phal chen): pas//de la chags dang bcing pa med pa'i phyir//chags med sems ni 'jug par mi 'gyur te//'jug par byed pa'i dbang yang dmigs pa med//
284a7(phal chen): chos kyi lus ni rab tu thob byas shing //'gro ba kun la mtshan nyid ji bzhin pa//chos nyid de la mtshan nyid med gsungs pa//de 'dra'i mtshan nyid chos 
284a8(phal chen): la rab tu zhugs//de ltar de dag 'grogs pa bsam mi khyab//de la sems ni lhag ma med par byed//bsam yas chos rnams kun la rab tu zhugs//
284b1(phal chen): sems ni sems min rnam par dben par shes//de dag chos dben nyid la 'jug byed cing //de ltar chos nyid rjes su sems pa byed//g.yo dang blom sems thams cad 
284b2(phal chen): 'gags pa ste//de phyir yon tan rnam par grags pa yin//de dag rnam smin thams cad ma lus par//ye shes rgyas btab zad par mi 'gyur te//de ni mi zad mi 
284b3(phal chen): bzang nyid kyi dngos//mi zad mtha' yi tshul dang 'gal ba med//bsam pa lus kyi phyi rol yod pa min//sems ni nang na'ang yod pa ma yin te/
284b4(phal chen): dus rnams gang na'ang sems ni yod pa min//brlom sems rlom chos med pas zhi bar 'gyur//de 'dra'i smon lam ldan pa rgyal sras rnams//chos 
284b5(phal chen): kyi rang bzhin zhi ba stong pa ste//chos rnams ma lus thams cad g.yo ba med//bdag nyid rgyal bas rnam par sangs rgyas med//de bzhin nyid gang 'gro ba kun 
284b6(phal chen): mnyam pa'i//chos nyid mtshan nyid rjes su shes byas shing //bsam yas mtshan nyid de dag mthong gyur nas//mtshan nyid med pa'i shes la rmongs pa 
284b7(phal chen): med//de ltar zab pa'i spyod la rab brtson pa//de dag byang chub rtag par brtson pa ste//de dag smon lam de yang bzlog pa med//'gro ba kun gyi don du 
284b8(phal chen): de dag brtson//de dag 'jig rten rnams las 'das gyur cing //'chi 'pho yongs su rtog pa med byas te//sprul pa 'dra bar sems kyang rig byas nas//
